# Skills · P · part 47

850 entries, pi-remote to pidgeon. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pi-remote**: Put a new LeandrOS kernel on the Raspberry Pi 5 over the network and run it with no SD-card swap - upload the rpi5 build into the root, kexec into it, verify, promote. A bad kernel is recovered by a power cycle (the firmware always boots the card's kernel.img, which this flow never writes). Use for every rpi5 kernel change once the Pi has network. ([forain/leandros](https://github.com/forain/leandros/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pi-remote) · ★3)
- **pi-rescue**: Forward an investigation, explicit fix request or follow-up to pi in print mode and return its answer verbatim, with one resumable rescue session per checkout. Use for /pi:rescue; not for reviews of local changes (/pi:review) or multi-issue dispatch (/pi:dispatch). ([nq-rdl/agent-extensions](https://github.com/nq-rdl/agent-extensions/tree/HEAD/skills/pi-rescue))
- **pi-research**: Multi-agent web research for any coding agent. Use for anything requiring current information from the internet — news, trends, product/service/company facts, statistics, people, places, events, APIs, library behavior, or 'what is X / how does X work' questions. Also use to search a local knowledge store of previously researched findings before doing live research. Powered by the pi-research engine (search, stealth scraping, security databases, Stack Exchange). Not for reading local files, running project commands, or analyzing this repository's own source. ([Lincoln504/pi-research](https://github.com/Lincoln504/pi-research/tree/HEAD/agent-skill/pi-research) · ★65)

([IlhanTech/plugin-pi-cc](https://github.com/IlhanTech/plugin-pi-cc/tree/HEAD/plugins/pi/skills/pi-result-handling))

- **pi-review**: Use pi-review only when the user explicitly requests an independent review or review workflow: code review, review of a diff or MR, loop review, review-fix-re-review closeout, panel review gates, or review status/progress questions. Do not use it for repository inspection, spec-driven evaluation or optimization (for example, checking whether X can be optimized against a specification), implementation-oriented analysis, diagnosis, migration, or conformance fixes (for example, checking compliance and correcting the implementation) unless the user separately asks for an independent review. ([ZephyrDeng/pi-review](https://github.com/ZephyrDeng/pi-review/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/pi-review) · ★16 · 2 copies)
- **pi-review**: Run an adversarial, multi-reviewer code review of a diff using pi subagents, then apply the valid fixes yourself. Use whenever the user asks to review code or a change — "review this PR", "review our/the code", "review your work so far", "double check the code", "check my work", "look over this", "any bugs in this?", "sanity check this" — or when finalizing a non-trivial change. Requires pi-subagents. ([ahmadaccino/skillaccinos](https://github.com/ahmadaccino/skillaccinos/tree/HEAD/skills/pi-review) · ★1)
- **pi-review**: Run a read-only pi code review of local git changes with the OpenAI Codex review rubric and return prioritized findings verbatim. Use for /pi:review on a working tree or branch diff; not for focus-text reviews, fixes, or non-OpenAI prompt tuning. ([nq-rdl/agent-extensions](https://github.com/nq-rdl/agent-extensions/tree/HEAD/skills/pi-review))
- **pi-review-loop**: Use when you want a fresh-context code-review gate before committing — runs Pi only with the approved OpenAI Codex GPT-6.2 Sol model (`gpt-6.2-sol`) at medium (or, on request, high) reasoning over the current git diff in a bounded, observable loop. Never falls back to Claude/Anthropic, local models, OpenRouter, or another provider. Drives review → fix → re-review under the value-driven stopping rules in cross-agent-review-cycle. Triggers: "have pi review this", "pi review before commit", "run the pi review loop". ([ephes/agent-stuff](https://github.com/ephes/agent-stuff/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/pi-review-loop) · ★1)
- **pi-review-loop**: Run automated iterative review (N passes) to reduce missed regressions after edits or before shipping. ([panamini/pi-tooling](https://github.com/panamini/pi-tooling/tree/HEAD/skills/pi-review-loop))
- **pi-review-pr**: Adversarial cross-model review of a GitHub PR or architecture plan/blueprint via the local pi-review CLI driving pi. Use for PR review, cross-model review, second-model review, plan review, blueprint review, or architecture-document review — including GitHub PR URLs, local Markdown files, and Notion blueprint links. Oracle is used only when Gabriel explicitly requests it. ([Gabrielgvl/herdr-tools](https://github.com/Gabrielgvl/herdr-tools/tree/HEAD/herdr-profiles/profile-plugins/manager/skills/pi-review-pr) · 2 copies)
